      228 +fn format_procedure_hover(proc: &al_syntax::ProcedureInfo) -> 
          +String {                                                      
      229 +    let local = if proc.is_local { "local " } else { "" };    
      230 +    let params: Vec<String> = proc

          +se { "" }, p.name, p.type_name))                              
      234 +        .collect();                                           
      235 +    let params_str = params.join("; ");                       
      236 +    let return_str = proc                                     
      237 +        .return_type                                          
      238 +        .as_ref()                                             
      239 +        .map(|r| format!(": {}", r))                          
      240 +        .unwrap_or_default();                                 
      241 +    format!("```al\n{}procedure {}({}){}\n```", local, proc.na
          +me, params_str, return_str)                                   
      242 +}

      244 +fn format_symbol_hover(entry: &al_symbols::SymbolEntry) -> Str
          +ing {                                                         
      245 +    let mut lines = Vec::new();                               
      246 +    let id_str = if entry.id != 0 { format!(" {}", entry.id) }
          + else { String::new() };                                      
      247 +    lines.push(format!("```al\n{}{} \"{}\"\n```", entry.kind, 
          +id_str, entry.name));                                         
      248 +    lines.push(format!("*({} package)*", entry.package));     
      249 +    if !entry.fields.is_empty() {                             
      250 +        lines.push(format!("\n**Fields:** {}", entry.fields.le
          +n()));                                                        
      251 +    }                                                         
      252 +    if !entry.methods.is_empty() {                            
      253 +        let method_names: Vec<&str> = entry.methods.iter()    
      254 +            .filter(|m| !m.is_local)                          
      255 +            .take(8)                                          
      256 +            .map(|m| m.name.as_str())                         
      257 +            .collect();                                       
      258 +        if !method_names.is_empty() {                         
      259 +            lines.push(format!("\n**Methods:** {}", method_nam
          +es.join(", ")));                                              
      260 +        }                                                     
      261 +    }                                                         
      262 +    if !entry.enum_values.is_empty() {                        
      263 +        let values: Vec<&str> = entry.enum_values.iter().take(
          +10).map(|v| v.name.as_str()).collect();                       
      264 +        lines.push(format!("\n**Values:** {}", values.join(", 
          +")));                                                         
      265 +    }                                                         
      266 +    lines.join("\n")                                          
      267 +}

      102 +fn make_rename_text(node_kind: &str, original_text: &str, new_
          +name: &str) -> String {

      104 +        || (original_text.starts_with('"') && original_text.en
          +ds_with('"'));

      107 +        format!("\"{}\"", clean)

      109 +        new_name.to_string()

      247 +fn count_params(detail: &str) -> usize {                      
      248 +    let start = match detail.find('(') { Some(i) => i + 1, Non
          +e => return 0 };                                              
      249 +    let end = match detail.rfind(')') { Some(i) => i, None => 
          +return 0 };                                                   
      250 +    if start >= end { return 0; }                             
      251 +    let inner = detail[start..end].trim();                    
      252 +    if inner.is_empty() { return 0; }                         
      253 +    let mut count = 1usize;                                   
      254 +    let mut depth = 0i32;                                     
      255 +    for ch in inner.chars() {                                 
      256 +        match ch {                                            
      257 +            '(' | '[' | '<' => depth += 1,                    
      258 +            ')' | ']' | '>' => depth -= 1,                    
      259 +            ';' if depth == 0 => count += 1,                  
      260 +            _ => {}                                           
      261 +        }                                                     
      262 +    }                                                         
      263 +    count                                                     
      264 +}                                                             
      265 +                                                              
      266 +fn finalize_completion_items(items: &mut Vec<CompletionEntry>)
          + {                                                            
      267 +    let mut seen = std::collections::HashSet::new();          
      268 +    items.retain(|item| seen.insert(item.label.to_lowercase())
          +);                                                            
      269 +                                                              
      270 +    for item in items.iter_mut() {                            
      271 +        if item.sort_text.is_some() { continue; }             
      272 +        let is_callable = matches!(item.kind, CompletionKind::
          +Function | CompletionKind::Method);                           
      273 +        if is_callable {                                      
      274 +            let pc = item.detail.as_deref().map(count_params).
          +unwrap_or(0);                                                 
      275 +            item.sort_text = Some(format!("1_{pc:02}_{}", item
          +.label.to_lowercase()));                                      
      276 +        } else {                                              
      277 +            item.sort_text = Some(format!("1_{}", item.label.t
          +o_lowercase()));                                              
      278 +        }                                                     
      279 +    }                                                         
      280 +                                                              
      281 +    items.sort_by(|a, b| {                                    
      282 +        a.sort_text.as_deref().unwrap_or(a.label.as_str())    
      283 +            .cmp(b.sort_text.as_deref().unwrap_or(b.label.as_s
          +tr()))                                                        
      284 +            .then_with(|| a.label.to_lowercase().cmp(&b.label.
          +to_lowercase()))                                              
      285 +    });                                                       
      286 +}                                                             
      287 +                                                              
      288 +/// Convert a tower-lsp CompletionItem to our transport-agnost
          +ic type.                                                      
      289 +fn from_lsp_completion(item: tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionI
          +tem) -> CompletionEntry {                                     
      290 +    let kind = match item.kind {                              
      291 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::KEYWORD
          +) => CompletionKind::Keyword,                                 
      292 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::SNIPPET
          +) => CompletionKind::Snippet,                                 
      293 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::FIELD) 
          +=> CompletionKind::Field,                                     
      294 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::PROPERT
          +Y) => CompletionKind::Property,                               
      295 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::METHOD)
          + => CompletionKind::Method,                                   
      296 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::FUNCTIO
          +N) => CompletionKind::Function,                               
      297 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::VARIABL
          +E) => CompletionKind::Variable,                               
      298 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::CLASS) 
          +=> CompletionKind::Class,                                     
      299 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::MODULE)
          + => CompletionKind::Module,                                   
      300 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::ENUM) =
          +> CompletionKind::Enum,                                       
      301 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::ENUM_ME
          +MBER) => CompletionKind::EnumMember,                          
      302 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::VALUE) 
          +=> CompletionKind::Value,                                     
      303 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::STRUCT)
          + => CompletionKind::Struct,                                   
      304 +        Some(tower_lsp::lsp_types::CompletionItemKind::REFEREN
          +CE) => CompletionKind::Reference,                             
      305 +        _ => CompletionKind::Text,                            
      306 +    };                                                        
      307 +    let documentation = item.documentation.map(|d| match d {  
      308 +        tower_lsp::lsp_types::Documentation::String(s) => s,  
      309 +        tower_lsp::lsp_types::Documentation::MarkupContent(m) 
          +=> m.value,                                                   
      310 +    });                                                       
      311 +    CompletionEntry {                                         
      312 +        label: item.label,                                    
      313 +        kind,                                                 
      314 +        detail: item.detail,                                  
      315 +        documentation,                                        
      316 +        insert_text: item.insert_text,                        
      317 +        sort_text: item.sort_text,                            
      318 +    }                                                         
      319 +}                                                             
      320 +                                                              
      321 +#[cfg(test)]                                                  
      322 +mod tests {                                                   
      323 +    use super::*;                                             
      324 +                                                              
      325 +    #[test]                                                   
      326 +    fn count_params_works() {                                 
      327 +        assert_eq!(count_params("()"), 0);                    
      328 +        assert_eq!(count_params(""), 0);                      
      329 +        assert_eq!(count_params("(A: Text)"), 1);             
      330 +        assert_eq!(count_params("(A: Text; B: Integer)"), 2); 
      331 +        assert_eq!(count_params("(A: Text; B: Integer; C: Bool
          +ean)"), 3);                                                   
      332 +        assert_eq!(count_params("(A: List of [Text]; B: Intege
          +r)"), 2);                                                     
      333 +        assert_eq!(count_params("(A: Text): Boolean"), 1);    
      334 +    }                                                         
      335 +}

      163 +        edit: Some(WorkspaceEdit { changes: vec![(uri.clone(),
          + edits)] }),                                                  
      164 +        is_preferred: false,                                  
      165 +    }                                                         
      166 +}                                                             
      167 +                                                              
      168 +fn detect_indent(text: &str, line: u32) -> String {           
      169 +    if let Some(line_str) = text.lines().nth(line as usize) { 
      170 +        let indent_len = line_str.len() - line_str.trim_start(
          +).len();                                                      
      171 +        line_str[..indent_len].to_string()                    
      172 +    } else {                                                  
      173 +        "    ".to_string()                                    
      174 +    }                                                         
      175 +}                                                             
      176 +                                                              
      177 +fn compute_pascal_case_fix(text: &str, range: tower_lsp::lsp_t
          +ypes::Range) -> Option<TextEdit> {                            
      178 +    let lines: Vec<&str> = text.lines().collect();            
      179 +    let line = range.start.line as usize;                     
      180 +    if line >= lines.len() { return None; }                   
      181 +    let start_col = range.start.character as usize;           
      182 +    let end_col = range.end.character as usize;               
      183 +    let line_text = lines[line];                              
      184 +    if end_col > line_text.len() || start_col >= end_col { ret
          +urn None; }                                                   
      185 +    let name = line_text[start_col..end_col].trim_matches('"')
          +;                                                             
      186 +    if name.is_empty() { return None; }                       
      187 +    let mut chars = name.chars();                             
      188 +    let first = chars.next()?;                                
      189 +    let fixed = format!("{}{}", first.to_uppercase(), chars.as
          +_str());                                                      
      190 +    Some(TextEdit { range: range.into(), new_text: fixed })   
      191 +}                                                             
      192 +                                                              
      193 +#[allow(deprecated)]                                          
      194 +fn source_action_add_doc_comment(                             
      195 +    workspace: &Workspace,                                    
      196 +    uri: &Url,                                                
      197 +    text: &str,                                               
      198 +    range: tower_lsp::lsp_types::Range,                       
      199 +) -> Option<CodeActionEntry> {                                
      200 +    let (_, tree) = crate::parsing::get_or_parse(&workspace.do
          +cuments, uri)?;                                               
      201 +    let doc_symbols = al_syntax::extract_document_symbols(&tre
          +e, text);                                                     
      202 +                                                              
      203 +    for sym in &doc_symbols {                                 
      204 +        if let Some(children) = &sym.children {               
      205 +            for child in children {                           
      206 +                if child.kind != tower_lsp::lsp_types::SymbolK
          +ind::FUNCTION { continue; }                                   
      207 +                if range.start.line >= child.range.start.line 
      208 +                    && range.start.line <= child.range.end.lin
          +e                                                             
      209 +                {                                             
      210 +                    let proc_line = child.range.start.line as 
          +usize;                                                        
      211 +                    if proc_line > 0 {

          +llect();                                                      
      213 +                        if proc_line <= lines.len() {         
      214 +                            let prev_line = lines[proc_line.sa
          +turating_sub(1)].trim();                                      
      215 +                            if prev_line.starts_with("///") { 
          +return None; }

          +ge.start.line);

      220 +                    let param_names = parse_parameter_names_fr
          +om_detail(params_detail);

          + indent);

      223 +                    doc.push_str(&format!("{}/// </summary>\n"
          +, indent));                                                   
      224 +                    for param in &param_names {

          +me=\"{}\">Description.</param>\n", indent, param));           
      226 +                    }

      228 +                        if detail.contains("):") || detail.con
          +tains(") :") {                                                
      229 +                            doc.push_str(&format!("{}/// <retu
          +rns>Description of return value.</returns>\n", indent));      
      230 +                        }                                     
      231 +                    }                                         
      232 +                    let edit = TextEdit {                     
      233 +                        range: Range {                        
      234 +                            start: super::Position { line: chi
          +ld.range.start.line, character: 0 },                          
      235 +                            end: super::Position { line: child
          +.range.start.line, character: 0 },                            
      236 +                        },                                    
      237 +                        new_text: doc,                        
      238 +                    };                                        
      239 +                    return Some(CodeActionEntry {             
      240 +                        title: "Add procedure documentation".t
          +o_string(),                                                   
      241 +                        kind: CodeActionKind::Refactor,       
      242 +                        edit: Some(WorkspaceEdit { changes: ve
          +c![(uri.clone(), vec![edit])] }),                             
      243 +                        is_preferred: false,                  
      244 +                    });                                       
      245 +                }                                             
      246 +            }                                                 
      247 +        }                                                     
      248 +    }                                                         
      249 +    None                                                      
      250 +}                                                             
      251 +                                                              
      252 +fn source_action_add_region(uri: &Url, text: &str, range: towe
          +r_lsp::lsp_types::Range) -> Option<CodeActionEntry> {         
      253 +    let indent = detect_indent(text, range.start.line);       
      254 +    let region_start = TextEdit {                             
      255 +        range: Range {                                        
      256 +            start: super::Position { line: range.start.line, c
          +haracter: 0 },                                                
      257 +            end: super::Position { line: range.start.line, cha
          +racter: 0 },                                                  
      258 +        },                                                    
      259 +        new_text: format!("{}//region MyRegion\n", indent),   
      260 +    };                                                        
      261 +    let region_end = TextEdit {                               
      262 +        range: Range {                                        
      263 +            start: super::Position { line: range.end.line + 1,
          + character: 0 },                                              
      264 +            end: super::Position { line: range.end.line + 1, c
          +haracter: 0 },                                                
      265 +        },                                                    
      266 +        new_text: format!("{}//endregion\n", indent),         
      267 +    };                                                        
      268 +    Some(CodeActionEntry {                                    
      269 +        title: "AL: Wrap in region".to_string(),              
      270 +        kind: CodeActionKind::Refactor,                       
      271 +        edit: Some(WorkspaceEdit { changes: vec![(uri.clone(),
          + vec![region_start, region_end])] }),                         
      272 +        is_preferred: false,                                  
      273 +    })                                                        
      274 +}                                                             
      275 +                                                              
      276 +fn parse_parameter_names_from_detail(detail: &str) -> Vec<Stri
          +ng> {                                                         
      277 +    let start = match detail.find('(') { Some(i) => i + 1, Non
          +e => return vec![] };                                         
      278 +    let end = match detail.rfind(')') { Some(i) => i, None => 
          +return vec![] };                                              
      279 +    if start >= end { return vec![]; }                        
      280 +    let inner = &detail[start..end];                          
      281 +    inner.split(';')                                          
      282 +        .filter_map(|param| {                                 
      283 +            let trimmed = param.trim();                       
      284 +            let name_part = trimmed.strip_prefix("var ").unwra
          +p_or(trimmed);                                                
      285 +            name_part.split(':').next().map(|n| n.trim().to_st
          +ring())                                                       
      286 +        })                                                    
      287 +        .filter(|n| !n.is_empty())                            
      288 +        .collect()                                            
      289 +}

       25 +fn collect_inlay_hints(                                       
       26 +    node: tree_sitter::Node<'_>,                              
       27 +    source: &[u8],                                            
       28 +    text: &str,                                               
       29 +    tree: &tree_sitter::Tree,                                 
       30 +    workspace: &Workspace,                                    
       31 +    doc_symbols: &[DocumentSymbol],                           
       32 +    range: &Range,                                            
       33 +    hints: &mut Vec<InlayHint>,                               
       34 +) {                                                           
       35 +    let node_start = node.start_position().row as u32;        
       36 +    let node_end = node.end_position().row as u32;            
       37 +    if node_end < range.start.line || node_start > range.end.l
          +ine { return; }                                               
       38 +                                                              
       39 +    if node.kind() == "argument_list" || node.kind() == "call_
          +arguments" {                                                  
       40 +        if let Some(parent) = node.parent() {                 
       41 +            let call_info = extract_call_info(parent, source);
       42 +            if let Some((func_name, receiver_name)) = call_inf
          +o {                                                           
       43 +                let position = Position {                     
       44 +                    line: node.start_position().row as u32,   
       45 +                    character: node.start_position().column as
          + u32,                                                         
       46 +                };                                            
       47 +                let arg_types = infer_argument_types(node, sou
          +rce, text, tree, position);                                   
       48 +                let param_names = lookup_parameter_names(     
       49 +                    workspace, doc_symbols, &func_name, receiv
          +er_name.as_deref(),                                           
       50 +                    text, tree, position, &arg_types,         
       51 +                );                                            
       52 +                if !param_names.is_empty() {                  
       53 +                    add_parameter_hints(node, source, &param_n
          +ames, hints);                                                 
       54 +                }                                             
       55 +            }                                                 
       56 +        }                                                     
       57 +    }                                                         
       58 +                                                              
       59 +    let mut cursor = node.walk();                             
       60 +    for child in node.children(&mut cursor) {                 
       61 +        collect_inlay_hints(child, source, text, tree, workspa
          +ce, doc_symbols, range, hints);

       63 +}

      141 +fn score_overload(candidate: &OverloadCandidate, arg_types: &[
          +Option<InferredType>]) -> u32 {                               
      142 +    let arg_count = arg_types.len();                          
      143 +    let mut score = 0u32;                                     
      144 +    if candidate.types.len() == arg_count { score += 1000; }  
      145 +    else if candidate.types.len() > arg_count { score += 100; 
          +}                                                             
      146 +    for (i, arg_type) in arg_types.iter().enumerate() {       
      147 +        if let Some(param_type_str) = candidate.types.get(i) {
      148 +            if let Some(inferred) = arg_type {                
      149 +                let (param_base, param_subtype) = parse_type_s
          +tring(param_type_str);                                        
      150 +                if param_base.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&inferred.b
          +ase) {                                                        
      151 +                    score += 50;                              
      152 +                    if let (Some(p_sub), Some(a_sub)) = (param
          +_subtype, inferred.subtype.as_deref()) {                      
      153 +                        if p_sub.eq_ignore_ascii_case(a_sub) {
          + score += 25; }                                               
      154 +                    }                                         
      155 +                }                                             
      156 +            }                                                 
      157 +        }                                                     
      158 +    }                                                         
      159 +    score                                                     
      160 +}

      162 +fn select_best_overload(candidates: &[OverloadCandidate], arg_
          +types: &[Option<InferredType>]) -> Option<Vec<String>> {      
      163 +    candidates.iter().max_by_key(|c| score_overload(c, arg_typ
          +es)).map(|c| c.names.clone())                                 
      164 +}

      166 +fn extract_call_info(node: tree_sitter::Node<'_>, source: &[u8
          +]) -> Option<(String, Option<String>)> {                      
      167 +    match node.kind() {                                       
      168 +        "member_call_suffix" | "scope_call_suffix" => {       
      169 +            let member = node.child_by_field_name("member")?; 
      170 +            let method_name = member.utf8_text(source).unwrap_
          +or("").trim_matches('"').to_string();                         
      171 +            let receiver = extract_receiver_before(node, sourc
          +e);                                                           
      172 +            Some((method_name, receiver))                     
      173 +        }                                                     
      174 +        "call_suffix" => {                                    
      175 +            if let Some(prev) = node.prev_sibling() {         
      176 +                let name = prev.utf8_text(source).unwrap_or(""
          +).trim_matches('"').to_string();                              
      177 +                return Some((name, None));                    
      178 +            }                                                 
      179 +            None

      182 +            let mut cursor = node.walk();                     
      183 +            for child in node.children(&mut cursor) {

          +ntifier" || kind == "name" {

          +"");                                                          
      187 +                    return Some((t.trim_matches('"').to_string
          +(), None));

      189 +            }

      195 +fn extract_receiver_before(suffix_node: tree_sitter::Node<'_>,
          + source: &[u8]) -> Option<String> {                           
      196 +    let prev = suffix_node.prev_sibling()?;                   
      197 +    match prev.kind() {                                       
      198 +        "primary_expression" => Some(prev.utf8_text(source).un
          +wrap_or("").trim_matches('"').to_string()),                   
      199 +        "member_suffix" | "member_call_suffix" => {           
      200 +            let member = prev.child_by_field_name("member")?; 
      201 +            Some(member.utf8_text(source).unwrap_or("").trim_m
          +atches('"').to_string())                                      
      202 +        }                                                     
      203 +        _ => None,                                            
      204 +    }                                                         
      205 +}                                                             
      206 +                                                              
      207 +#[allow(deprecated)]                                          
      208 +#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]                          
      209 +fn lookup_parameter_names(                                    
      210 +    workspace: &Workspace,                                    
      211 +    doc_symbols: &[DocumentSymbol],                           
      212 +    func_name: &str,                                          
      213 +    receiver_name: Option<&str>,                              
      214 +    text: &str,                                               
      215 +    tree: &tree_sitter::Tree,                                 
      216 +    position: Position,                                       
      217 +    arg_types: &[Option<InferredType>],                       
      218 +) -> Vec<String> {                                            
      219 +    // 1. Local procedures                                    
      220 +    let mut candidates: Vec<OverloadCandidate> = Vec::new();  
      221 +    for sym in doc_symbols {                                  
      222 +        if let Some(children) = &sym.children {               
      223 +            for child in children {                           
      224 +                if child.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(func_name) 
      225 +                    && (child.kind == SymbolKind::FUNCTION || 
          +child.kind == SymbolKind::EVENT)                              
      226 +                {                                             
      227 +                    if let Some(detail) = &child.detail {     
      228 +                        let params = parse_parameters_from_det
          +ail(detail);                                                  
      229 +                        if !params.is_empty() {               
      230 +                            candidates.push(OverloadCandidate 
          +{                                                             
      231 +                                names: params.iter().map(|(n, 
          +_)| n.clone()).collect(),                                     
      232 +                                types: params.iter().map(|(_, 
          +t)| t.clone()).collect(),                                     
      233 +                            });                               
      234 +                        }                                     
      235 +                    }                                         
      236 +                }                                             
      237 +            }                                                 
      238 +        }                                                     
      239 +    }                                                         
      240 +    if !candidates.is_empty() {                               
      241 +        if let Some(best) = select_best_overload(&candidates, 
          +arg_types) { return best; }                                   
      242 +    }                                                         
      243 +                                                              
      244 +    // 2. Receiver type resolution                            
      245 +    if let Some(recv) = receiver_name {                       
      246 +        if let Some(names) = lookup_via_receiver(workspace, fu
          +nc_name, recv, text, tree, position, arg_types) {             
      247 +            return names;                                     
      248 +        }                                                     
      249 +    }                                                         
      250 +                                                              
      251 +    // 3. Package symbols                                     
      252 +    let symbols = workspace.symbols.search(func_name, 5);     
      253 +    let candidates: Vec<OverloadCandidate> = symbols.iter()   
      254 +        .flat_map(|e| e.methods.iter())                       
      255 +        .filter(|m| m.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(func_name))   
      256 +        .map(|m| OverloadCandidate {                          
      257 +            names: m.parameters.iter().map(|p| p.name.clone())
          +.collect(),                                                   
      258 +            types: m.parameters.iter().map(|p| p.type_name.clo
          +ne()).collect(),                                              
      259 +        }).collect();                                         
      260 +    if let Some(best) = select_best_overload(&candidates, arg_
          +types) { return best; }                                       
      261 +                                                              
      262 +    // 4. Builtins                                            
      263 +    let builtins = workspace.builtins.read().unwrap().clone();
      264 +    let candidates: Vec<OverloadCandidate> = builtins.iter()  
      265 +        .flat_map(|bt| bt.methods.iter())                     
      266 +        .filter(|m| m.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(func_name))   
      267 +        .map(|m| OverloadCandidate {                          
      268 +            names: m.parameters.iter().map(|p| p.name.clone())
          +.collect(),                                                   
      269 +            types: m.parameters.iter().map(|p| p.type_name.clo
          +ne()).collect(),                                              
      270 +        }).collect();                                         
      271 +    if let Some(best) = select_best_overload(&candidates, arg_
          +types) { return best; }                                       
      272 +                                                              
      273 +    // 5. Embedded builtins                                   
      274 +    if let Some(names) = lookup_embedded_builtin(func_name) { 
          +return names; }                                               
      275 +                                                              
      276      Vec::new()
      277  }
      278 +                                                              
      279 +fn lookup_via_receiver(                                       
      280 +    workspace: &Workspace, func_name: &str, receiver_name: &st
          +r,                                                            
      281 +    text: &str, tree: &tree_sitter::Tree, position: Position, 
      282 +    arg_types: &[Option<InferredType>],                       
      283 +) -> Option<Vec<String>> {                                    
      284 +    let resolver = al_syntax::TypeResolver::new(tree, text);  
      285 +    let decl = resolver.resolve_type(receiver_name, position)?
          +;                                                             
      286 +                                                              
      287 +    // Builtins filtered by receiver type                     
      288 +    let builtins = workspace.builtins.read().unwrap().clone();
      289 +    let candidates: Vec<OverloadCandidate> = builtins.iter()  
      290 +        .filter(|bt| bt.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&decl.type_n
          +ame)                                                          
      291 +            || decl.type_subtype.as_deref().is_some_and(|s| bt
          +.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(s)))                               
      292 +        .flat_map(|bt| bt.methods.iter())                     
      293 +        .filter(|m| m.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(func_name))   
      294 +        .map(|m| OverloadCandidate {                          
      295 +            names: m.parameters.iter().map(|p| p.name.clone())
          +.collect(),                                                   
      296 +            types: m.parameters.iter().map(|p| p.type_name.clo
          +ne()).collect(),                                              
      297 +        }).collect();                                         
      298 +    if let Some(best) = select_best_overload(&candidates, arg_
          +types) { return Some(best); }                                 
      299 +                                                              
      300 +    // Package symbols by resolved subtype                    
      301 +    if let Some(subtype) = &decl.type_subtype {               
      302 +        let candidates: Vec<OverloadCandidate> = workspace.sym
          +bols.get_by_name(subtype).iter()                              
      303 +            .flat_map(|e| e.methods.iter())                   
      304 +            .filter(|m| m.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(func_name)
          +)                                                             
      305 +            .map(|m| OverloadCandidate {                      
      306 +                names: m.parameters.iter().map(|p| p.name.clon
          +e()).collect(),                                               
      307 +                types: m.parameters.iter().map(|p| p.type_name
          +.clone()).collect(),                                          
      308 +            }).collect();                                     
      309 +        if let Some(best) = select_best_overload(&candidates, 
          +arg_types) { return Some(best); }                             
      310 +    }                                                         
      311 +                                                              
      312 +    // Workspace objects by resolved subtype                  
      313 +    if let Some(subtype) = &decl.type_subtype {               
      314 +        let obj_key = subtype.to_lowercase();                 
      315 +        if let Some(file_path) = workspace.workspace_objects.g
          +et(&obj_key) {                                                
      316 +            let file_path = file_path.value().clone();        
      317 +            if let Some(file_text) = workspace.workspace_files
          +.get(&file_path) {                                            
      318 +                let content = file_text.value();              
      319 +                let result = AlParser::parse_quick(content);  
      320 +                let target_symbols = al_syntax::extract_docume
          +nt_symbols(&result.tree, content);                            
      321 +                let mut candidates: Vec<OverloadCandidate> = V
          +ec::new();                                                    
      322 +                for sym in &target_symbols {                  
      323 +                    if let Some(children) = &sym.children {   
      324 +                        for child in children {               
      325 +                            if child.name.eq_ignore_ascii_case
          +(func_name)                                                   
      326 +                                && (child.kind == SymbolKind::
          +FUNCTION || child.kind == SymbolKind::EVENT)                  
      327 +                            {                                 
      328 +                                if let Some(detail) = &child.d
          +etail {                                                       
      329 +                                    let params = parse_paramet
          +ers_from_detail(detail);                                      
      330 +                                    if !params.is_empty() {   
      331 +                                        candidates.push(Overlo
          +adCandidate {                                                 
      332 +                                            names: params.iter
          +().map(|(n, _)| n.clone()).collect(),                         
      333 +                                            types: params.iter
          +().map(|(_, t)| t.clone()).collect(),                         
      334 +                                        });                   
      335 +                                    }                         
      336 +                                }                             
      337 +                            }                                 
      338 +                        }                                     
      339 +                    }                                         
      340 +                }                                             
      341 +                if let Some(best) = select_best_overload(&cand
          +idates, arg_types) { return Some(best); }                     
      342 +            }                                                 
      343 +        }                                                     
      344 +    }                                                         
      345 +                                                              
      346 +    lookup_embedded_builtin(func_name)                        
      347 +}                                                             
      348 +                                                              
      349 +fn lookup_embedded_builtin(func_name: &str) -> Option<Vec<Stri
          +ng>> {                                                        
      350 +    let names: &[&str] = match func_name.to_lowercase().as_str
          +() {                                                          
      351 +        "message" | "error" => &["Value"],                    
      352 +        "confirm" => &["Question"],                           
      353 +        "strmenu" => &["OptionString"],                       
      354 +        "format" => &["Value"],                               
      355 +        "strlen" | "maxstrlen" | "get" | "findset" | "findfirs
          +t" | "findlast"                                               
      356 +        | "getposition" | "count" | "isempty" | "reset" | "set
          +recfilter" => return Some(vec![]),                            
      357 +        "copystr" => &["Position", "Length"],                 
      358 +        "selectstr" => &["Number", "CommaString"],            
      359 +        "strpos" => &["SubString"],                           
      360 +        "contains" | "startswith" | "endswith" => &["Value"], 
      361 +        "setrange" => &["FieldNo", "FromValue", "ToValue"],   
      362 +        "setfilter" => &["FieldNo", "String"],                
      363 +        "insert" | "modify" | "delete" => &["RunTrigger"],    
      364 +        "fieldno" => &["FieldName"],                          
      365 +        "setposition" => &["Position"],                       
      366 +        "read" | "write" => &["Value"],                       
      367 +        "run" | "setrecord" | "getrecord" => &["Record"],     
      368 +        _ => return None,                                     
      369 +    };                                                        
      370 +    Some(names.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect())       
      371 +}                                                             
      372 +                                                              
      373 +fn parse_parameters_from_detail(detail: &str) -> Vec<(String, 
          +String)> {                                                    
      374 +    let trimmed = detail.trim();                              
      375 +    let start = match trimmed.find('(') { Some(i) => i + 1, No
          +ne => return Vec::new() };                                    
      376 +    let mut depth = 1;                                        
      377 +    let mut end = start;                                      
      378 +    for (i, ch) in trimmed[start..].char_indices() {          
      379 +        match ch { '(' => depth += 1, ')' => { depth -= 1; if 
          +depth == 0 { end = start + i; break; } } _ => {} }            
      380 +    }                                                         
      381 +    let params_str = &trimmed[start..end];                    
      382 +    if params_str.trim().is_empty() { return Vec::new(); }    
      383 +    params_str.split(';').filter_map(|param| {                
      384 +        let param = param.trim();                             
      385 +        if param.is_empty() { return None; }                  
      386 +        let param = param.strip_prefix("var ").unwrap_or(param
          +).trim();                                                     
      387 +        if let Some(colon_pos) = param.find(':') {            
      388 +            let name = param[..colon_pos].trim().trim_matches(
          +'"');                                                         
      389 +            let type_name = param[colon_pos + 1..].trim();    
      390 +            if !name.is_empty() { return Some((name.to_string(
          +), type_name.to_string())); }                                 
      391 +        }                                                     
      392 +        let name = param.trim().trim_matches('"');            
      393 +        if !name.is_empty() { Some((name.to_string(), String::
          +new())) } else { None }                                       
      394 +    }).collect()                                              
      395 +}                                                             
      396 +                                                              
      397 +fn add_parameter_hints(                                       
      398 +    arg_list: tree_sitter::Node<'_>,                          
      399 +    _source: &[u8],                                           
      400 +    param_names: &[String],                                   
      401 +    hints: &mut Vec<InlayHint>,                               
      402 +) {                                                           
      403 +    let expr_parent = arg_list.children(&mut arg_list.walk()) 
      404 +        .find(|c| c.kind() == "expression_list")              
      405 +        .unwrap_or(arg_list);                                 
      406 +    let mut cursor = expr_parent.walk();                      
      407 +    let mut arg_idx = 0;                                      
      408 +    for child in expr_parent.children(&mut cursor) {          
      409 +        let kind = child.kind();                              
      410 +        if !child.is_named() || kind == "comma" || kind == "("
          + || kind == ")" || kind == "semicolon" { continue; }          
      411 +        if arg_idx >= param_names.len() { break; }            
      412 +        hints.push(InlayHint {                                
      413 +            position: Position {                              
      414 +                line: child.start_position().row as u32,      
      415 +                character: child.start_position().column as u3
          +2,                                                            
      416 +            },                                                
      417 +            label: InlayHintLabel::String(format!("{}:", param
          +_names[arg_idx])),                                            
      418 +            kind: Some(InlayHintKind::PARAMETER),             
      419 +            text_edits: None,                                 
      420 +            tooltip: None,                                    
      421 +            padding_left: None,                               
      422 +            padding_right: Some(true),                        
      423 +            data: None,                                       
      424 +        });                                                   
      425 +        arg_idx += 1;                                         
      426 +    }                                                         
      427 +}
